[Q1] RAPACIOUSNESS
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 10/12/2024 (Morning) ]]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) generosity
(b) voracity
(c) greed
(d) violence
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Generosity-thequalityofbeing kind and giving. 
• Rapaciousness - aggressive greed or grasping behavior. Voracity -excessive eagerness or hunger 
• Greed - an intense desireformorethan one needs.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'RAPACIOUSNESS' का अर्थ (अति-लोभ / लालच (Greed / voracity)) होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'generosity' (उदारता / दानशीलता) इसका सटीक विलोम शब्द (Antonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) generosity: उदारता / दानशीलता • (B) voracity • (C) greed • (D) violence

[Q12] GRAPPLE [
[EXAM: SSC MTS 12/11/2024 (Morning) ]]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Wrestle
(b) Grumble
(c) Clasp
(d) Release
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Repulse - to drive back or cause strong dislike.Entice - to attract or tempt by offering pleasure or advantage.Allure - to attract or charm.Invite - to ask someone to come or participate.Tangle - to twist together in a confusing mass. (b) 
• Steady - firm, stable, and not changing. 
• Fluctuate - to change or vary irregularly. 
• Descend - to move downward or fall.Mitigate - to make less severe or serious.Wobble - to move unsteadily from side to side. (a) 
• Vibrant - full of energy, brightness, and life. 
• Lifeless - lacking energy, vitality, or color. 
• Spiritual - relating to the soul or religious belief.Ashless - not containing ash.Muted - (of a sound or voice) quiet and soft. (a) 
• In different - having no interest or concern. 
• Inquisitive - showing curiosity or a strong desire to learn. 
• Uncanny - strange or mysterious, often in an unsettling way. Antonyms 
• Indeterminate - not exactly known, established, or defined. 
• Intelligent - having or showing high mental capacity. (d) 
• Release - to let go or set free. 
• Grapple - to struggle or wrestle with something physically or mentally. 
• Wrestle - to fight or struggle with someone or something. 
• Grumble - to complain or express dissatisfaction. 
• Clasp - to hold tightly or firmly. SSC CGL 2024 Tier - 1
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: '[' का अर्थ  होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'Grumble' (बड़बड़ाना) इसका सटीक विलोम शब्द (Antonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) Wrestle • (B) Grumble: बड़बड़ाना • (C) Clasp • (D) Release

[Q15] ALLAY
[EXAM: SSC CGL 09/09/2024 (2 n d Shift)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) mitigate
(b) simplify
(c) mollify
(d) aggravate
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Aggravate - make (a problem, injury, or offence) worse or more serious. 
• Allay - to reduce excitement or emotion. 
• Mitigate - make (something bad) less severe, serious, or painful. 
• Simplify - make (something) simpler or easier to do or understand. 
• Mollify - appease the anger or anxiety of (someone).
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'ALLAY' का अर्थ  होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'aggravate' (छेड़ना) इसका सटीक विलोम शब्द (Antonym) है।

(a) fickle
(b) stable
(c) mischievous
(d) volatile
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Stable - not likely to change or fail. 
• Capricious - given to sudden changes in mood or behavior. 
• Fickle - changing frequently, especially in regard to loyalties or affections. 
• Mischievous - causing or showing a fondness for causing trouble in a playful way. 
• Volatile - likely to change rapidly and un predictably, especially for the worse. (b)

[Q140] CONTEMPLATE

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) deviate
(b) understate
(c) follow up
(d) overlook
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Overlook - to fail to see or notice something. 
• Contemplate - to consider one particular thing for a long time in a serious and quiet way. 
• Deviate - to change direction suddenly. 
• Understate - to describe something in a way that makes it seem less important.

• Pessimistic - expecting the worst or seeing the negative side.

• Desperate - feeling or showing a hopeless sense of urgency or despair.

• Arid - extremely dry or having little or no rain.Damp - slightly wet. 
• Misty - full of, covered with, or surrounded by mist.

• Soaked - something thoroughly wet. (d) 
• Fortuitous - happening by accident or chance rather than intention.Catastrophic - causing sudden great damage or suffering.

• Redemption - the action of saving or being saved from sin, error, or evil consequences. 
• Corrugation - the process of shaping into parallel grooves or ridges.

[Q677] INORDINATE
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 16/04/2021 (Morning)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) exorbitant
(b) irrational
(c) disproportionate
(d) reasonable
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Reasonable - acceptable and appropriate in a particular situation 
• Inordinate - much greater than usual or expected 
• Exorbitant - much more expensive than it should be 
• Irrational - not based on reason or clear thought 
• Disproportionate - too large or too small when compared to something else (d)

[Q730] ABUNDANT
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Plentiful
(b) Scanty
(c) Ample
(d) Copious
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Abundant' means existing or available in large quantities; the exact opposite is 'scanty', which means small or insufficient in quantity.
उदा. 'Abundant' का अर्थ प्रचुर मात्रा में होता है, इसका विलोम शब्द 'scanty' (अल्प या कम) है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Adjective Antonyms

[Q731] TRANSITORIERS
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Permanent
(b) Temporary
(c) Fleeting
(d) Transient
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Transitory' means lasting for a short time; its exact opposite is 'Permanent', which means lasting or remaining unchanged indefinitely.
उदा. 'Transitory' का अर्थ क्षणिक होता है, जिसका विपरीत शब्द 'Permanent' (स्थाई) है।
[RULE] Prefix / Root Analysis

[Q732] MITIGATE
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Aggravate
(b) Alleviate
(c) Relieve
(d) Assuage
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Mitigate' means to make less severe or painful. Its antonym is 'Aggravate', which means to make a problem or bad situation worse.
उदा. 'Mitigate' का अर्थ कम करना होता है, जबकि 'Aggravate' का अर्थ स्थिति को और बदतर या गंभीर बनाना है।
[RULE] Action Verbs Antonyms

'Candid' means truthful and straightforward; frank. The opposite is 'Secretive', meaning inclined to conceal thoughts and feelings.

Mitigate means to make less severe, serious, or painful. 'Aggravate' means to make a problem or injury worse, which is the opposite.
उदा. Mitigate का अर्थ किसी चीज़ के प्रभाव को कम करना होता है। 'Aggravate' इसका विलोम है जिसका अर्थ स्थिति को और अधिक गंभीर या बदतर बनाना है।
[RULE] Prefix/Root Analysis

उदा. 'Fastidious' (अति सावधान/नखरेबाज) का विपरीतार्थक 'Slapdash' (लापरवाही से किया गया) है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Antonym

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]

[SOL]
'Stagnant' means showing no activity; dull and sluggish (often referring to water or air). Its antonym is 'Flowing', moving along steadily and continuously.
उदा. 'Stagnant' (ठहरा हुआ) का विलोम शब्द 'Flowing' (बहता हुआ) है।

[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Opaque
(b) Clear
(c) Lucid
(d) Exposed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Transparent means clear and easily seen through. The opposite is 'opaque', meaning not able to be seen through. उदाहरण: The window was transparent, but the wall was opaque.
[RULE] Antonym

---

[Q1002] Pervasive
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Limited
(b) Conspicuous
(c) Transitory
(d) Trivial
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: *Pervasive* means spreading throughout or affecting everything. Its opposite is *Limited* meaning restricted in scope or extent.
उदाहरण: "The problem was pervasive across all departments." – "The issue was limited to the marketing department."
[RULE] Antonym

---

[Q1003] Sparse
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Abundant
(b) Scanty
(c) Meager
(d) Thin
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: *Sparse* means thinly scattered or scarce. The opposite is *Abundant* meaning plentiful.
उदाहरण: "The forest was sparse with trees." – "The forest was abundant with trees."
[RULE] Antonym

---

[Q1004] Enigmatic
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Transparent
(b) Obscure
(c) Mysterious
(d) Cryptic
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: *Enigmatic* means mysterious or puzzling. Its antonym is *Transparent* meaning clear and easily understood.
उदाहरण: "The solution was enigmatic." – "The explanation was transparent."
[RULE] Antonym

---

[Q1005] Recalcitrant
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Obedient
(b) Defiant
(c) Stubborn
(d) Uncooperative
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: *Recalcitrant* means disobedient or unwilling to cooperate. The opposite is *Obedient* meaning compliant.
उदाहरण: "The child was recalcitrant to the rules." – "The child was obedient to the rules."
[RULE] Antonym

---

[Q1006] Eminent
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Obscure
(b) Famous
(c) Renowned
(d) Notable
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: *Eminent* means famous or respected. Its opposite is *Obscure* meaning unknown or not well known.
उदाहरण: "He is an eminent scientist." – "He is an obscure researcher."
[RULE] Antonym

[Q1007] Cumbersome

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Lightweight
(b) Bulky

(d) Clumsy
[ANS] a

उदाहरण: "The equipment was cumbersome to move." – "The equipment was lightweight to move."

[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Transparent
(b) Murky
(c) Dim
(d) Cloudy
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: *Opaque* means not allowing light to pass through, not clear. Its opposite is *Transparent* meaning clear and see-through.

[Q1009] Placate

[ANS] a

[Q1012] Choose the word most opposite in meaning to the given word: ABUNDANT
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Plentiful
(b) Meagre
(c) Ample
(d) Generous
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Abundant means existing or available in large quantities; plentiful. Its antonym is 'Meagre', which means lacking in quantity or quality.
उदा. Abundant का अर्थ प्रचुर मात्रा में होता है। इसका विपरीतार्थक शब्द 'Meagre' (अल्प या कम) है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Antonym

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Permanently
(b) Briefly
(c) Temporarily
(d) Fleeing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Transitorily means for a brief time. Its antonym is 'Permanently', meaning lasting or remaining without change indefinitely.

[Q1014] Choose the correct antonym of: MITIGATE
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) Alleviate
(b) Relieve
(c) Aggravate
(d) Lessened
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Mitigate means to make less severe, serious, or painful. Its antonym is 'Aggravate', which means to make a problem or offense worse.
उदा. Mitigate का अर्थ कम करना होता है। इसका विलोम 'Aggravate' (बदतर करना या बढ़ाना) है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Antonym

---

[Q1015] OBSCURE
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Vague
(b) Clear
(c) Uncertain
(d) Hidden
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Obscure means not discovered or known about; uncertain. Its antonym is 'Clear', meaning easy to perceive, understand, or interpret.
उदा. Obscure का अर्थ अस्पष्ट होता है। इसका विपरीतार्थक शब्द 'Clear' (स्पष्ट) है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Antonym

---

[Q1016] Choose the word most opposite in meaning to: NADIR
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) Zenith
(b) Base
(c) Bottom
(d) Nadir
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Nadir means the lowest point in the fortunes of a person or organization. Its antonym is 'Zenith', which means the time at which something is most powerful or successful (highest point).
उदा. Nadir का अर्थ सबसे निचला स्तर होता है, और 'Zenith' उसका सर्वोच्च बिंदु होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Antonym

---

(b) Adverse

Hostile means showing or feeling opposition or dislike; unfriendly. Its antonym is 'Amicable', meaning characterized by friendliness and absence of discord.
उदा. Hostile का अर्थ शत्रुतापूर्ण होता है। इसका विपरीत 'Amicable' (मैत्रीपूर्ण) है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Antonym

'Fecund' means producing or capable of producing an abundance of offspring or new growth; fertile. Its antonym is 'Barren' (unproductive).
उदा. 'Fecund' का अर्थ उपजाऊ या उत्पादक होता है, जिसका विलोम 'Barren' (बंजर) है।
[RULE] Vocabulary - Advanced Adjectives

[Q1095] IMPECCABLE
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Flawless
(b) Faultless
(c) Imperfect
(d) Spotless
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Impeccable' means faultless, in accordance with the highest standards. Its antonym is 'Imperfect', meaning not perfect or containing flaws.
उदा. 'Impeccable' का अर्थ त्रुटिहीन होता है, जिसका विलोम 'Imperfect' (अपूर्ण या त्रुटिपूर्ण) है।

(c) Senile
(d) Young
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Juvenile' means youthful or relating to young people. Its antonym is 'Senile', meaning showing the weaknesses or diseases of old age.
उदा. 'Juvenile' का अर्थ किशोर या युवा होता है, जिसका विपरीतार्थक शब्द 'Senile' (बुढ़ापे से संबंधित/सठियाया हुआ) है।

[Q1097] scarce
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Abundant
(b) Limited
(c) Rarer
(d) Insufficient
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Scarce means not plentiful or in short supply. Its antonym is abundant, which means plentiful or plentifully available.
उदा. 'The desert had scarce water, but the oasis offered abundant life.'
[RULE] Opposite meaning words

[Q1098] strict
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Lenient
(b) Ruthless
(c) Harsh
(d) Stern
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Strict refers to a rigid or harsh enforcement of rules. Lenient means permissive or forgiving, which is the opposite.
उदा. 'The teacher was strict, but the substitute was lenient with the assignments.'
[RULE] Opposite meaning words

[Q1099] decrease
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Reduce
(b) Lower
(c) Increase
(d) Diminish
[ANS] c
[SOL]
"Decrease" means to become smaller or smaller in amount. "Increase" means to become larger or larger in amount, which is its antonym.
उदा. 'The population decreased, but the workforce increased during the boom.'

(c) Resolve
(d) Admit
[ANS] c

Compromise means to settle a dispute by mutual concession. Resolve means to find a solution without giving up, which is opposite.
उदा. 'They compromised on the budget, but the council resolved to reject the proposal.'

(d) Positive

"Optimistic" means hopeful about the future. "Pessimistic" means having a negative outlook, which is its antonym.

(b) Aggravate

An antonym expresses the opposite notion. 'Conservative' means cautious or traditional, whereas 'progressive' means forward‑looking or reformative.
उदाहरण के लिये, 'conservative' का मतलब रूढ़िवादी या परंपरागत होता है, जबकि 'progressive' का मतलब प्रगतिशील या सुधारवादी होता है।
[RULE] Antonyms / Opposite meaning

💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: 'narrow' का अर्थ  होता है। दिए गए विकल्पों में से 'wide'  इसका सटीक विलोम शब्द (Antonym) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) wide • (B) tight: कसा हुआ • (C) slim: छरहरा • (D) thin
